# Italian (it) second-batch fan-out (ACTIVE, started 2026-08-23)

**What:** the parallel-to-de/es/fr/pt program — fan the ~114 EN-only lcs-shell second-batch
activities (games-build-walk output, commit `12327ce5`) out to **Italian (it)**, ONE per plan-mode
session, via a **3-agent native Italian ensemble** (K-1 scuola-primaria matematica/alfabetizzazione
pedagogue + linguist + B2C/SEO content creator), **rebuild not translate** for the **Indicazioni
nazionali** (scuola primaria, classe prima–terza). Sibling of the de/es/fr/pt fan-outs — **same
#1..#114 order**, same recipe, Italian curriculum/language.

**DEPLOY POLICY (operator 2026-08-23):** build ALL it activities **LOCALLY**; **commit + push only,
NO deploy per activity** (accumulate on `pivot/printable-business-toolkit`); deploy the whole it set
in ONE batch at the very end (§20.4 cp-before-build chain, one `git pull` grabs all commits).

**Operator cadence:** operator asks to switch to plan mode before each → EnterPlanMode → 3-agent
native it ensemble (content into the plan file) → ExitPlanMode approval → build → full LOCAL DoD
(§A.13.62) → show operator local screenshots → commit+push (NO deploy). ONE at a time.

**Locked rulings (whole program):**
1. **rebuild, not translate** — genuinely sound for an Italian classroom; native 3-agent ensemble.
2. **NEVER skip** — find the Italian teaching point for EVERY activity incl. English-specific literacy.
3. Build order mirrors de/es/fr/pt: math/measurement/geometry first, literacy rebuilt when reached.
4. **Character names KEPT** across locales (Tilly, …). In-app title vs SEO page_title may differ.
5. **Framework NAME only, NO code** in it-facing text: cite **"le Indicazioni nazionali"** (or omit).
   NEVER "Common Core", NEVER a raw CCSS/curriculum code. The CCSS code stays hidden machine anchor
   only (`alignment.code` / JSON-LD `targetName` / `/standards/<code>`).

**it is a FULL locale — infra already present (no scaffolding):**
- `EDUCATIONAL_FRAMEWORK_BY_LOCALE.it = 'Indicazioni nazionali'` (page.tsx ~L470); hreflang it→it.
- `frontend/messages/activity-content/it.json` prose container + Italian `templates` exist. Gate =
  **`scripts/verify-activity-content-it.js`** (≥50 it activities; about≥2/practices≥3/howToPlay≥2/
  learningGoals≥2; unique about[0]; no `{placeholder}`; no "Common Core"; no raw code; register:
  "materiali manipolabili" NOT the anglicism "manipolativi").
- `strand-names.ts` it entries EXIST (Numeri / Spazio e figure / **Misure e dati** / Lettura /
  Consapevolezza fonologica). ⚠ **`GRADE_OVERRIDE`/`STRAND_OVERRIDE` had ZERO it entries** before #1
  (all targeted de/fr/es/pt).
- **⚠ STRAND-NAME CALQUE (surfaced #1, operator-approved per-activity fix):** the shared
  `strand-names.ts` it `Measurement & Data → "Misure e dati"` is a **Common Core calque, NOT an
  Indicazioni nazionali nucleo**. The three real nuclei (scuola primaria math) are **Numeri / Spazio
  e figure / Relazioni, dati e previsioni** — misura lives in the LAST. Every it MEASUREMENT activity
  hits this. **Operator ruled per-activity STRAND_OVERRIDE for now** (strand-names.ts NOT touched, to
  avoid relabeling already-shipped it measurement activities). A locale-wide strand-names.ts fix is a
  DEFERRED future commission.
- **Missing it in strand-names.ts** (fall back to English → future literacy/fraction activities need
  a route STRAND_OVERRIDE or a strand-names it entry): Reading: Informational Text, Reading:
  Literature, Writing, Language, Vocabulary Acquisition and Use, Number & Operations—Fractions.
- Italian grade LABELS live in `subject-hub.ts GRADE_COPY.it` ("la classe prima/seconda/terza") but
  the ACTIVITY page renders the raw manifest grade literal ("1"/"K"…), not "classe prima" — net-new
  wiring if ever wanted (out of scope, same as all locales).

**Per-activity edit recipe (activity-layer ONLY; `<engine>-core.js`/`lcs-shell.*`/Direction-A CSS = 0
lines, `git diff --name-only` proof):**
- `mini tools/<engine>-activity.js`: add `it` to the string maps (COLOR_L/L/strings). `txt()`/`clabel()`
  fall back to en. Add a short ensemble-note comment mirroring the sibling comment style.
- `mini tools/<engine>-activities.json`: add `it` to slug/page_title/page_intro. Slug ASCII-safe
  (`^[a-z0-9-]+$`, no accents), grade in slug, framework by NAME only, collision-check vs it slugs.
- `frontend/messages/activity-content/it.json`: add `prose[<id>]` under top-level `"prose"`
  (about≥2/practices≥3/howToPlay≥2/learningGoals≥2; unique about[0]; no code/Common-Core/{placeholder};
  "materiali manipolabili").
- `mini tools/<engine>-activity.html`: bump `<engine>-activity.js?v=N`.
- `frontend/app/[locale]/activities/[slug]/page.tsx`: add `GRADE_OVERRIDE`/`STRAND_OVERRIDE` `it`
  entries ONLY where the IT grade/nucleo ≠ the CCSS auto-map; bump `ACTIVITY_WRAPPER_VERSION`.
- **FORWARD RULE:** after ANY page.tsx GRADE/STRAND edit run `cd frontend && npx tsc --noEmit`
  (dup-key trap — MERGE `it:` into an existing `{de:…}` entry, don't add a separate object).
- ⚠ **NEVER edit `frontend/public/mini-tools/`** — gitignored STALE copy; source of truth = `mini tools/`.

**Gates per build (lean §A.13.62):** `node --check` + JSON-valid both files +
`verify-activity-content-it.js` + `preflight-activity-routes.js` (PLAIN — NOT `--all-locales`, since
nl/sv/da/no/fi aren't fanned out yet) + `visual-qa-activity.js --activity=<id> --locale=it` (320·360·
412·768·1024·1366 × every round) + EN-regression `--locale=en` then `--locale=it` (restore shots) +
EU-tell/EN-leak grep + personal Read 768/1024/360 (desktop-first) + `git diff --name-only` 0-core →
commit+push, NO deploy.

   graduati = classe 2/3 = trust-killer, ⚠ articolo "LO spago" s impura → dello/allo); object = **"il

   MASCULINE in COLOR_L (Rosso/Blu/Verde/Giallo/Viola/Arancione — only Rosso/Giallo inflect, land masc.);

7. **graph-it `bar-graph.2-md-d-10` (commit `03045a6b`)** — „La staccionata di Pip" / slug
   `diagramma-a-barre-leggere-e-costruire-classe-seconda`. wrapper 9.460, graph-it-activity.js?v=9 (core
   ?v=1 UNTOUCHED). **FIRST it DATA activity.** Mascot **Pip lo scoiattolo** kept. **classe SECONDA**
   (2.MD.D.10; leggere+costruire un grafico a barre = classe seconda; NO GRADE_OVERRIDE — grade '2' auto,
   all siblings grade 2). **STRAND = «Relazioni, dati e previsioni» — the 1st it STRAND_OVERRIDE MERGE into
   an existing multi-locale entry** (added `it:` to the graph-it `{de,fr,es,pt}` entry, page.tsx L199); the
   auto-map «Misure e dati» is the CC calque + a DOMAIN ERROR for a data activity (de „Daten und Häufigkeit"
   / fr „Organisation et gestion de données" / es „Análisis de datos" / pt „Probabilidade e estatística"
   made the same fix). Even CLEANER than the #1/#2 measurement override — the nucleo literally names *dati*.
   Purely additive engine: `CAT_L.it` (6) + `L.it` (16) + `it` on the 9 `strings` keys (locale via
   `global.LCS.i18n.current`, no LANG var). **Categories = forest set KEPT** (same as de/es/fr/pt): foglie /
   bacche / ghiande / **pigne** (⚠ BARE — «pigna» = pinecone unambiguously in it; pineapple = «ananas», so NO
   gloss, unlike es «piñas de pino» / pt «pinhas de pinheiro») / funghi / fiori. Chart term = **«grafico a
   barre»** (primary; «diagramma a barre» variant; pedagogue REJECTED «istogramma» = continuous data +
   «ideogramma»/«pittogramma» = icon form); tally = **«conteggio» / «tabella dei dati»**; fence flavour =
   **«staccionata»** (f). **⚠ Comparative gender crux (funghi/fiori m, rest f; engine = ONE template per
   prompt → can't select quante/quanti per category) → SOLVED via the it PARTITIVE SHIELD** (es/pt/fr
   pattern): **«Quante ce ne sono in più di {a} che di {b}?»** — «Quante» agrees with an implicit fem
   «quantità», «ce ne sono» invariant, category quarantined behind «di {a}» → gender-safe for masc+fem.
   qFewer/qTotal same shape; qVerify **«Ci sono {by} {x} in più di {y}. Vero o falso?»** (invariant «Ci sono»
   + number+bare-noun). ⚠ the manifest's more/fewer/verify rounds only ever put the FEMININE categories
   (leaf/berry/acorn/pinecone) in the {a}/{b}/{x}/{y} slots — funghi/fiori appear only as standalone bar
   labels — so there's ZERO masc-agreement exposure in the shipped rounds anyway; the shield is
   belt-and-suspenders. **Scope caution (pedagogue):** copy stays on *leggere/confrontare dati* — NO claim of
   previsioni/probabilità/statistica (nucleo NAME as the alignment label is fine); SEO agent's
   learningGoals[2] «pensiero statistico» was DROPPED. **Known-benign (surfaced-not-acted):** qVerify with
   `{by}=1` would render «1 funghi» (number-agreement wart, «1 fungo» correct) — but the verify round is
   `by:2` (acorn vs pinecone) → UNREACHABLE; a PRE-EXISTING cross-locale limitation pt/es/fr/de share (no
   singular/plural noun branching without a core change). §A.13.62 GREEN it 84/84 + en 84/84; tsc page clean

The 4 -o→-a = canonical «plurali particolari»; amico/amica = the memorable no-h vs +h velar pair. **⚠ THE +s FRAMING (reconciled):** Italian NEVER pluralizes with -s, so the hard-coded `+"s"` chip is reframed as the FOREIGN/English plural (uovos/amicos) an Italian meets in inglese/spagnolo → winNote/nudges teach «in italiano il plurale non si fa con la -s: cambia in modo speciale» (genuine classe-2 concept; pedagogue: GOOD fit, no salvage). **GRADE classe seconda — NO override** (native '2'): singolare/plurale is classe 1ª but plurali particolari + the velar h-rule (-chi/-ghi, amico/amici) are classe 2ª. **⭐ STRAND — REGISTERED `strand-names.ts` `Language.it = 'Riflessione linguistica'`** (Indicazioni area «Elementi di grammatica esplicita e riflessione sugli usi della lingua»; parallels es «Reflexión sobre la lengua» / fr «Étude de la langue»; **REUSABLE by EVERY future it literacy activity — no per-activity STRAND_OVERRIDE**, page.tsx has no plural entry). **CHARACTER «Perla»** (localize «Pearl», ensemble-unanimous, no fork; «stagno» = pond, consistent w/ #3/#9/#19). **VOCAB/punct:** «...» caporali · «Qual è il plurale di «X»?» (❌ NEVER «qual'è») · win «Sì! …» · hear «Ascolta». Correct = chip whose tense===round.time, **DERIVED not stored** (`isAnswer`). ⚠ CORE (`tense-core.js`, 0 lines): round = `{time,timeWord,subject,verb:{lemma,forms:{present,past,future},regular}}`; the 3 `forms` strings ARE the chips (must be 3-distinct). The core's `facts()` checks (`futureWellFormed:/^will/`, `presentIsBare:/s$/`) are **ENGLISH-only** (EN build gate `verify-tense-core.js`); siblings shipped without it-gating → it validation = visual-qa + node-eval, NOT verify-tense-core. `local-test-tense.js` is EN-only (hardcoded lang=en). **⭐ THE it PAST-TENSE FORK (native ensemble, all 3 agreed): PAST = passato prossimo with AVERE** (2-word chip «hanno giocato») — the everyday completed past a classe-2 kid uses + matches «Ieri». **passato remoto** (giocarono) REJECTED (not spoken in most of Italy, not a classe-2 production target — the genuine it fork the OTHER Romance sibs didn't face: their single-word completed past — es indefinido/pt perfeito — has NO spoken classe-2 Italian single-word equivalent). **imperfetto** (giocavano) REJECTED (aspectually habitual, wrong for a completed «Ieri»; muddies the 3-way contrast). **ALL 9 verbs constrained to AVERE + invariable participle** → no essere-agreement. Honest tradeoff (pedagogue): the past chip is the only 2-word chip = a mild length cue, but the nudges redirect to the TIME WORD; do NOT "fix" w/ single-word past (sacrifices correctness for a cosmetic decoy). **9 rounds (3 past/3 present/3 future), all AVERE + regular + 3-distinct (node-eval GREEN):** le rane nuotano/hanno nuotato/nuoteranno · i bambini giocano/hanno giocato/**giocheranno** (-care +h) · gli uccelli cantano/hanno cantato/canteranno · i cani mangiano/hanno mangiato/**mangeranno** (-giare drop i) · i gatti **dormono**/hanno dormito/**dormiranno** (⚠ the ONLY -ire: -ono present + -iranno future, NOT *dormano/*dormeranno) · le maestre lavorano · gli amici disegnano · le bambine ascoltano · i nonni guardano. **Subjects repinned by pedagogue:** nuotare→le rane (NOT i gatti — cats don't swim); disegnare→gli amici (dropped i pittori — thin vocab + they dipingono). **GRADE classe seconda** (GRADE_OVERRIDE `it:'2'`, mirrors de/fr/es/pt: systematic 3-way contrast WITH futuro = classe 2; classe 1 = verb incidental). **STRAND auto «Riflessione linguistica»** (Language.it registered #20; NO override). **CHARACTER «Juniper» kept** (fr/es/pt all keep it; title «La torre del tempo di Juniper»). Window labels GRAMMATICAL **Passato/Presente/Futuro** (matches es/pt; NOT the poetic EN Before/Now/Soon). **VOCAB/punct:** «...» caporali · **«Quale parola va bene?»** (dodges «qual'è» — «Quale» needs no elision) · win «Sì! Questa parola è al tempo giusto!» · praise gender-neutral (never «Bravo»). Options: apply = array `[{affix,text}×3]`, which = `['un','re','ful']`. **⭐ THE it "OPPOSITE" PREFIX = `dis-`** (native ensemble decisive — pedagogue+linguist): dis- has ZERO allomorphy (one fixed cog label), a clean reverse/opposite sense pairing with ri-, the largest transparent classe-2 set. **`in-` REJECTED** (im-/il-/ir- allomorphy breaks a single label); **`s-` REJECTED** (single-letter cog thin + semantically noisy — sparire/sbagliare aren't "opposite"). Affixes: **dis- (IL CONTRARIO) / ri- (DI NUOVO) / -oso (PIENO DI)**. **⭐ REAL GRAMMAR FIX over the pt design (pedagogue):** the «pieno di {root}» decoy is NOUN-LOCKED — ungrammatical when the round's root is an adjective/verb («pieno di ordinato» ✗, «pieno di leggere» ✗; the pt sibling shipped the odd auto-frame). it authors each -oso decoy's text with a REAL noun («pieno di ordine», «pieno di libri», «pieno di attenzione») → all 24 apply cards grammatical. **ZERO engine change** (per-round `text` authoring). **11 rounds (8 apply + 3 which), node-eval GREEN:** apply dis-×3 (disordinato/ordinato, disubbidiente/ubbidiente, disattento/attento) + ri-×2 (rileggere/leggere, riscrivere/scrivere) + -oso×3 (coraggioso/coraggio, pauroso/paura, rumoroso/rumore); which×3 fresh roots (onesto/dis-, aprire/ri-, gioia/-oso). Opposite slot = all ADJECTIVES (NOT disfare — verb, clashes w/ ri-); ri- = transparent "do again" verbs (exclude riuscire/ricevere/ripetere); -oso = mass/abstract NOUNS (exclude goloso/famoso/nervoso; count-noun -oso like nuvoloso needs plural «pieno di nuvole»). **GRADE classe seconda — NO override** (manifest `alignment.grade='2'` native; chip «Seconda elementare»). **⭐ STRAND — REGISTERED `strand-names.ts` `Vocabulary Acquisition and Use`.it = «Lessico»** (Indicazioni «Acquisizione ed espansione del lessico ricettivo e produttivo»; the vocab≠grammar SPLIT — «Lessico» for VOCAB vs «Riflessione linguistica» [#20/#21] for GRAMMAR; parallels fr «Le lexique»/es «Ampliación del vocabulario»/pt «Ampliação do vocabulário»; auto-maps, **NO page.tsx override** — affix has zero page.tsx entry). **REUSABLE by every future it vocabulary activity.** **CHARACTER «Marigold» kept**; «...» caporali (NOT pt curly aspas); machine «macchina», cog «ingranaggio»; praise «Sì!» never «Bravo»; ⚠ typographic apostrophe ’ (U+2019) in the ONE JS elision `l’ingranaggio` (fr/de convention; JSON takes plain `'`). For it (all activity-layer): (1) **`fracWordIT(a,b)`** — Italian fraction words (`NUM_IT` un/due/tre… + `UNIT_IT` {2:mezzo,3:terzo,4:quarto,6:sesto,8:ottavo}; a=1→«un {u}»; a>1→«{num} {u -o→-i}» → un mezzo/due terzi/tre quarti/cinque sesti/tre ottavi) + a new arm in the `fracWord` chain; (2) **`UNIT_ART_IT`** (all-masculine {2:'un mezzo'…}) + an it arm in the pizza aria (aria «una pizza rotonda con {UNIT_ART_IT} colorato»); (3) **8 inline-ternary aria `it` arms** (order-bar/take-piece-back/mold±uneven/mold-wall/bar-in-{b}-parts/bar-half/pizza/finished-bar); (4) **speak ×2 it-IT** (⚠ **fixed the it→`'en-US'` TTS fallback bug**); (5) **16 `strings`** `it:`; (6) **`promptL10n.it`** (11). **⚠ APOSTROPHE SPLIT: the 16 strings + 8 aria + fracWordIT code in `fox-forge-activity.js` (JS) → 100% apostrophe-free** (en «Pip's»/«aren't»/«that's»/«let's» → «di Pip»/«non sono uguali»/«Troppi pezzi»/«Guardiamo ancora»/enclitic «Consegnala»/«colane»/«colalo»); the 11 promptL10n.it + SEO + prose in JSON → U+2019 FINE. **⭐ GRADE = classe TERZA — NO override (pedagogue DECISIVE).** Le frazioni sono introdotte in classe TERZA nelle Indicazioni (la frazione come parte di un intero, frazioni semplici, costruire una frazione); questa attività fa il lavoro entry-level (a/b, denominatori ∈ {2,3,4,6,8}, niente equivalenze/operazioni) = terza. **ALIGNS es 3.º + de Klasse 3 + manifest grade 3; ⚠ do NOT harmonize to fr CM1 / pt 4º ano** (loro rinviano l'a/b al grade 4; l'Italia no). **⭐ STRAND = «Numeri» via page.tsx STRAND_OVERRIDE (pedagogue CONFIRMED).** "Number & Operations—Fractions" has no Italian nucleo; le frazioni SONO numeri → «Numeri». Routed via a per-activity page.tsx override (like fr; added `it: 'Numeri'` to the existing `{ fr: 'Nombres et calcul' }` row L185), keeping **`strand-names.ts` UNTOUCHED** (de/es/pt keep their strand-names.ts entries: de «Zahlen und Operationen», es «Sentido numérico», pt «Números»). **⭐⭐ TERMINOLOGY — the pedagogue OVERRODE two of the linguist/copywriter's defaults (I seeded them, both wrong):** **forge (verb) = «colare»** (cast/pour into a mold — the REAL chocolate-casting verb) ⚠ **NOT «forgiare»** (= *metalworking*; chocolate is cast, not forged); **bar (the whole) = «la tavoletta»** (di cioccolato — THE Italian word for a chocolate bar) ⚠ **NOT «la barra»** (= a generic rod). Also: **«un mezzo» NOT «una metà»** (la frazione 1/2, not the portion/quantity); mold = «lo stampo», piece = «il pezzo», order = «ordinazione» (f, chrome — avoids «l'ordine»). **CHARACTER: Pip la volpe (keep Pip).** REGISTER: gender-NEUTRAL praise. - **#53 chuffer `chuffer.rail-decompose.k-oa-a-3` — «Lo scalo di Chuffer» — 33rd it, 16th it MATH (Numeri) — LOCAL, commit `2b21c27d`, NO deploy.** Pure activity-layer fan-out over the (already-generalized) number-decompose engine (0-touched `rail-decompose-core.js` [locale-neutral, no strings] + `chuffer-activity.js`, EN + de/es/fr/pt live). **il trenino Chuffer** — a load of casse is split between a **vagone azzurro** and a **vagone rosso**; child moves crates, SAYS the split («5 è 2 e 3»), pulls the coupler («Tira il gancio») to log it in the **Quaderno di viaggio**, finds ALL the ways to scomporre a number ≤10 into two parts. 9 rounds, sub-tasks: free-decompose/move-one-covary/hunt-missing/make-ten/equation-build/mirror(commutativity)/judge-route. K.OA.A.3 machine anchor. **⚠ MEDIUM edit surface — engine uses a `strings` object (21 keys) + per-locale number-word maps (`WORDS_xx`) + `speak()` TTS + inline `LANG ===` ternaries; NO roundsL10n/promptL10n.** For it (all activity-layer): (1) **`WORDS_IT`** (0–10, masculine-abstract cardinals uno/due/tre… — a decomposing number is genderless) + **`it` branch in `numWord()`**; (2) **speak() it-IT** in BOTH the LCSAudio + speechSynthesis chains (⚠ the recurring it→raw/en-US TTS fallback bug — the speechSynthesis chain fell to en-US); (3) **21 `strings` it:**; (4) **10 inline ternary it arms** (move-blue/label/move-red aria «sposta una cassa…» · car caps «Vagone azzurro/rosso» · spoken decomposition ` è `+` e ` → «cinque è due e tre» · judge claim «Chuffer dice:» · judge feedback isNew/dup · win spoken · route-book title). **⚠ APOSTROPHE: all it JS values (WORDS_IT + 21 strings + inline) 100% apostrophe-free** (no U+0027 AND no U+2019 — «qual è» is apocope NOT elision so it is correct + safe; reworded «ce l'abbiamo»→«questo modo lo abbiamo già», «di'»→«nomina»); SEO+prose JSON → U+2019 FINE. **⭐ GRADE = `GRADE_OVERRIDE it:'1'` classe PRIMA (pedagogue DECISIVE; added to L103 row `{de:'1',fr:'1',pt:'1'}`).** *Comporre e scomporre i numeri entro il 10, in più modi* = NAMED classe-prima «Numeri» objective; the scuola dell'infanzia stops at pre-numerical work. ALIGNS de/fr/pt; es stays K. **⭐ STRAND «Numeri» — AUTO, NO override.** `strand-names.ts` maps `'Operations & Algebraic Thinking'` → it «Numeri» (no algebra nucleus in the primaria); UNTOUCHED, NO page.tsx STRAND row. **⭐⭐ TERMINOLOGY (pedagogue DECISIVE):** decompose = **«scomporre»** / «comporre» (⚠ REJECT «dividere» = the division OP, a real misconception here; reject «separare»); **«il tutto e le parti»**; spoken **«5 è 2 e 3»** (copula **è** [accent load-bearing: è verb vs e conj] + conj **e** — ⚠ NOT «fa»/«più», that is the addition/composition direction); crate = **«cassa/casse»** (reject «scatola»); car = **«vagone»**, blue = **«vagone azzurro»** (pedagogue RULED azzurro over «blu» for the #3FA7D6 swatch: blu = navy) / red = **«vagone rosso»**; coupler = **«il gancio»**, pull = **«Tira il gancio»** (preferred over «aggancia» = joining cars); make-ten partners = **«amici del dieci»** (⚠ reject «coppie del dieci»); switchyard = **«scalo di smistamento»** (title short «lo scalo»). **⚠⚠ ROUTE-BOOK RECONCILIATION = «Quaderno di viaggio»:** the pedagogue REJECTED the linguist+copywriter's «quaderno delle rotte» — «rotte» is NAUTICAL/AVIATION, WRONG for rails (a train has tratte/percorsi, not rotte) — and ruled «diario di bordo»/«quaderno di viaggio»; I used **«Quaderno di viaggio»** (keeps «quaderno» so the linguist's «Nel quaderno…»/«sul quaderno» stay coherent, zero extra churn). **⭐ Consequently DROPPED «rotta» from the judge buttons too** → newRoute **«Modo nuovo!»** (was «Rotta nuova!») + gotIt **«Già fatto!»** (was «Questa la abbiamo già!») — the activity uses «modo» = way everywhere else, and the two buttons now MIRROR judgeHint «è NUOVO o già fatto?». **⚠ maketenHint reconciled to surface the locked term:** «Un vagone è chiuso: trova il suo amico del dieci!» (the linguist's «il compagno del 10» → «il suo amico del dieci», apostrophe-free via the possessive dodging «l'amico»). **⭐ COMMUTATIVITY:** mirror string «Stesse casse scambiate: {a} e {b}, O {b} e {a}!» / prose «2 e 3 è come 3 e 2 — cambia solo l'ordine dei vagoni». **CHARACTER «il trenino Chuffer»** (SVG = blue steam loco; matches pt «o trenzinho»); in-app title «Lo scalo di Chuffer». **REGISTER:** gender-neutral praise (never «Bravo/Brava»); cardinals genderless. it takes the auto-map). **⭐⭐ TERMINOLOGY (pedagogue DECISIVE):** MORE/FEWER = «di più»/«di meno»; SAME-relation → **«tanti quanti»** in a group SENTENCE but **«uguale»** as the one-word button/verdict (⚠ pedagogue override: not bare «uguale» in the group trio); spoken «5 è più di 3» (copula è + «più di», ⚠ NOT «maggiore di» = too formal); friend = **«amici»** (⚠ pedagogue override: NOT the diminutive «amichetti»); buddy/pair = «compagno», «fare/formare le coppie», unit «coppia»; left over = **«senza compagno»** (spaiato = enrichment only); pile/group = **«gruppo»** (⚠ pedagogue override toward «gruppo» over «mucchietto» on the INTERACTIVE surface; «mucchietto» kept only in the warm marketing prose); make-equal = «Rendili uguali»/«Pareggia»; **«L'occhio inganna»** (JS apostrophe-free «i tuoi occhi»). **4 reconciliations applied to the linguist's parallel-generated strings:** (a) promptBuildEqual + revealBust: mucchietto→gruppo (revealBust «mucchietto più corto»→«gruppo raccolto»); (b) revealSame gains «tanti quanti»: «Sono UGUALI: {n} tutti e due, tanti quanti! - **#56 star-stitcher `star-stitcher.connect-sequence.k-cc-a-2` — «Conta e accendi le stelle» — 36th it, 19th it MATH (Numeri) — LOCAL, commit `5c726cf8`, NO deploy.** Pure activity-layer fan-out over the (already-generalized) count-on engine (0-touched `connect-sequence-core.js` [⚠ NOT same-named as the skin] + `star-stitcher-activity.js`, EN + de/es/fr/pt live). **la lucciola** (firefly) flies among dark stars; ONE star is already lit showing the start number (e.g. 7, NOT always 1); child taps «Conta ancora» to fly to the next star saying the next number, accende each; the last wakes a hidden constellation-creature. The skill = **contare in avanti a partire da un numero dato senza ricominciare da 1 (count-on / la sovraconta — il ponte verso l'addizione)**. 7 rounds. Numbers ≤18. K.CC.A.2 machine anchor. **⚠ SMALL surface (12 strings) — engine uses `strings` (12) + per-locale CREATURE gender maps + speak() TTS + ONE inline ternary (`creaturePhrase`); NO roundsL10n; ⚠ NO number-word table** (LCSAudio `type:'number'` does digit→word). For it (all activity-layer): (1) **`CREATURE_IT`** map (7 article+noun phrases, gender-agreed) after CREATURE_PT + **`it` branch in `creaturePhrase()`** (fallback «una stella»); (2) **speak() it-IT** in BOTH the LCSAudio + speechSynthesis chains (⚠ the recurring it→raw/en-US TTS fallback bug — speechSynthesis fell to en-US); (3) **12 `strings` it:**. **⚠ APOSTROPHE: all it JS values (12 strings + CREATURE_IT + fallback) 100% apostrophe-free** (no U+0027 AND no U+2019 — «l'étoile»→«la stella accesa», «di'» imperative→«Conta»/«Tocca», setHint «da lì lei parte» dodges «dov'è»/«c'è»); SEO+prose JSON → U+2019 FINE. **⭐ GRADE = `GRADE_OVERRIDE it:'1'` classe PRIMA (pedagogue DECISIVE; added to L107 row `{de:'1',pt:'1'}`).** *Contare in avanti a partire da un numero dato (senza ricominciare da 1), la sequenza orale, entro il 18* = NAMED classe-prima «Numeri» objective (count-on = bridge to addition; ≤18 above the infanzia band); ALIGNS de/pt, es/fr keep K. **⭐ STRAND «Numeri» — AUTO, NO override.** `strand-names.ts` maps `'Counting & Cardinality'` → it «Numeri»; UNTOUCHED, NO page.tsx STRAND row (fr has a maternelle-GS override; it takes the auto-map). **⭐⭐ TERMINOLOGY (pedagogue DECISIVE — the VERB SPLIT is load-bearing):** count-on concept = «contare in avanti a partire da un numero dato» (⚠ **«la sovraconta» = TEACHER JARGON**, ONLY in the parent-facing prose, NEVER a child-facing JS string); "Count on" button = **«Conta ancora»**; firefly = **«la lucciola»** (f, UNNAMED). **⭐ WAKE/LIGHT SPLIT: the per-star button LIGHTS the star = «Accendila!»** (both experts: «le stelle si accendono» is the idiomatic Italian) — on the button + in countHint/hintCheck; **«svegliare» reserved for the creature reveal** (win «Hai svegliato {c}!») → a two-verb story (accendi le stelle → svegli una creatura). start label «Parti da qui». **⭐ CREATURE_IT (both experts CONFIRMED):** una volpe (f) · un gufo (m) · una balena (f) · un cervo (m) · un orso (m) · **un gatto** (m — ⚠ pedagogue override of the es feminine «gata») · **un uccellino** (m, diminutive). A **wondering owl** («il gufo») loves to guess jars: the child makes a **WISH** (a *stima*) on a 0–20 number line (honored, NEVER graded — "your guess is never wrong"), the **barattolo** spreads its **caramelle**, the child COUNTS each **a uno a uno** to FIND OUT, then wish-vs-truth is compared (same/more/fewer, DIRECTION only). Skill = **contare a uno a uno per rispondere a «quante sono?» a 20** + *stima della numerosità* + confronto (the estimate→count→compare loop). 7 rounds: line-8 · scatter-7 · array-12 · circle-9 · relational-10(off a ref) · predict-comparison-11(binary di più/di meno) · compare-two(A/B which-more). ALIGNS de/pt (grade 1); es/fr keep K. Precedent: #57 mochi-feast (same K.CC.B.5) → classe prima. **⭐ STRAND «Numeri» — AUTO, NO override (pedagogue DECISIVE — the "previsioni" FALSE-FRIEND ruled OUT).** `strand-names.ts` maps `'Counting & Cardinality'` → it «Numeri»; the graded spine is one-to-one COUNTING (the *stima*/*confronto* are also «Numeri» ops = confrontare e ordinare quantità); NOTHING is collected/classified/graphed → NOT «Relazioni, dati e previsioni» despite "previsioni" in that domain name (there = statistical forecasting over data). UNTOUCHED, NO page.tsx STRAND row. **⭐⭐ TERMINOLOGY (pedagogue DECISIVE):** sweets = **«le caramelle»** (⚠ RULED IN — a jar of generic candy IS caramelle; the #57 «dolcetti» rejection does NOT apply, no banana here; countable, warm, fem. plural) · jar = **«il barattolo»** (NOT «vasetto»/«vaso») · owl = **«il gufo»** (m; **⚠ ADJUDICATED generic descriptor, NO proper name** — the pedagogue's «Ugo il gufo» offer DECLINED: the strings never name the owl, the copywriter prose uses generic «il gufo», siblings de "Eulchen"/fr "la chouette"/es/pt use descriptors) · the honored wish = **«la tua idea»** (⚠ NEVER «un'idea» — apostrophe) · count one-by-one = **«a uno a uno»** · "how many?" = **«quante?»** (⚠ FEM plural, agrees w/ le caramelle — never «quanti» for the count question) · more «di più» / fewer «di meno» · which-more «quale ne ha di più?» · equal «uguali» / twins «gemelli» (m, agrees w/ barattoli) · number line «la linea dei numeri». **⚠ owl's hobby verb «indovinare» OK on the OWL, kept OFF the child's honored guess.** **⭐ CHARACTER = «il gufo»** (generic descriptor, no proper name — see adjudication above). **REGISTER:** gender-neutral praise (never «Bravo/Brava»; «Che scoperta!»/«Ora lo sappiamo!»/«Sì!»). - **#69 twinsies `twinsies.count-twin.k-cc-b-5` — «Gemelli — tanti quanti!» — 50th it, 31st it MATH (Numeri) — LOCAL, commit `ad86857f`, NO deploy.** Pure activity-layer fan-out over the (already-generalized) count-twin engine (0-touched `count-twin-core.js` [⚠ NOT same-named as the skin] + `twinsies-activity.js`, EN + de/fr/es/pt live). A **Twin-Sprite** (folletto) makes a pile of acorns and wants a TWIN — not the same picture, the same MANY (same quantity, different arrangement); the child COUNTS the pile by tapping each acorn, then BUILDS a twin with the **same number** on their own plate, then taps "We're twins!". 7 rounds: line-5 · scattered-7 · array-2×3 · cover-line-12 · **numeral-8** (read the digit, build 8) · hidden-scattered-9 · count-on-from-6. Numbers reach 12. Skill = **contare "quanti sono" e formare la stessa quantità** (cardinalità + conservazione del numero + leggere un numerale). K.CC.B.5 machine anchor. **⚠ MID surface — engine uses a `strings` object (14 keys) + a `speak()` TTS (`type:'number'` → NO number-word table) + 1 `LANG===` aria ternary (spriteSVG) + 2 inline `LANG===` compound arms (win-speak + done-eq); NO gender maps, NO roundsL10n.** For it (all activity-layer): (1) **speak() it-IT** in the speechSynthesis `u.lang` chain (⚠ the recurring it→en-US fallback; LCSAudio uses `LANG` directly → it→'it' OK); (2) **1 aria it arm** (spriteSVG «folletto gemello», short form no article — matches fr «lutin jumeau»/es «duende gemelo»); (3) **14 `strings` it:**; (4) **2 inline it arms** (win-speak L206 «{n} e {n} — gemelli!» + done-eq L216 « — gemelli!», joiner literal ` & `). **⚠ APOSTROPHE: all it JS values 100% apostrophe-free** («We're twins!»→«Siamo gemelli!»; «Twin-Sprite's pile»→«il mucchio del folletto»; «let's»→«contiamo»; «è»/«più»/«quantità» = accents). Added to the GRADE_OVERRIDE row `{ de: '1', pt: '1' }` → `{ …, it: '1' }`; en/fr/es stay base K (fr=GS, es=preescolar display). **⭐ STRAND = «Numeri» — AUTO, NO it override (strand-names.ts L37: C&C → it «Numeri»; pedagogue CONFIRMED — the arrangements line/scattered/array are a DECOY, NOT geometry → must NOT drag to «Spazio e figure»).** ⚠ NOTE: twinsies HAS a STRAND_OVERRIDE row but it is **fr-only** (`{ fr: 'Découvrir les nombres…' }`, the French GS-maternelle domain) — **it is NOT added** (it auto-maps to «Numeri»). `strand-names.ts` UNTOUCHED. **⭐ TERMINOLOGY (pedagogue+linguist):** Twin-Sprite **«il folletto gemello»** (m., warm fairy-tale K-1 word, no monster connotation) · twins «gemelli» · same quantity **«la stessa quantità»** (descriptive/prompt) + **«tanti quanti»** (standalone idiom — title+win) · pile «il mucchio di ghiande» (⚠ NOT «pila» = neat stack) · plate «il tuo piatto» · button «Siamo gemelli!». **1 title/win reconciliation (pedagogue-decisive):** «tanti quanti!» in the standalone exclamations (title «Gemelli — tanti quanti!» + win «Gemelli! Tanti quanti!»); linguist's «la stessa quantità» in the descriptive prompt (needs a complete phrase — «con tanti quanti» is elliptical). **1 visual-QA content fix:** countHint «...per contare il mucchio del folletto gemello.»→«Tocca ogni ghianda e conta il mucchio del folletto.» — the long form wrapped 3 lines and the in-scene `.tw-say` line-clamp:2 CLIPPED it at 320×640 (r1/r2/r3/r5/r6, 13px hidden); the short form fits 2 lines (content fix, never the gate). **REGISTER:** gender-neutral (never «Bravo/Brava»; «Sì!»/«Perfetto!»). Numbers reach 20 (teen 14). Skill = **contare "quanti sono" e scrivere/produrre il numerale corrispondente 0–20** (incluso lo zero). K.CC.A.3 machine anchor. **⚠ HEAVIEST surface — engine uses `strings` (27 keys) + per-locale `WORDS_xx` (number-word) + a per-locale `KIND_xx` NOUN MAP (4 treasure kinds × `{art,bare,pl,ct}`, gender-aware) + `numWord()` + `speak()` TTS (`type:'number'`) + 6 inline `LANG===` KIND/aria chains + 2 inline `LANG===` speak arms; NO gender-prompt maps beyond KIND, NO roundsL10n.** For it (all activity-layer): (1) **`WORDS_IT`** (0–20) after WORDS_PT — REQUIRED (`speak(numWord(m.count))` speaks minted numerals); (2) **`KIND_IT`** after KIND_PT (acorn `{una ghianda, ghianda, le ghiande, contata}` · button `{un bottone, bottone, i bottoni, contato}` · firefly `{una lucciola, lucciola, le lucciole, contata}` · marble `{una biglia, biglia, le biglie, contata}` — art=indef article+noun for aria, bare=noun for parade aria, pl=DEF article+plural for subset prompt, ct=counted participle agreeing in gender); (3) **numWord it arm**; (4) **speak() it-IT** in the speechSynthesis `u.lang` chain (⚠ the recurring it→en-US fallback; LCSAudio uses `LANG` → it→'it' OK); (5) **27 `strings` it:**; (6) **6 inline KIND/aria it arms** (subset prompt `.pl` L162/L305 «Conta solo le ghiande!» · full-ten aria L210 «una decina completa — tocca per contarli tutti e dieci» · obj aria `{art}, {ct}` L221/L244 «una ghianda, contata»/«un bottone, contato» · parade aria `{bare} passa — tocca per contare` L256); (7) **2 inline speak it arms** (mintedZero L319 «zero» · pageDone L330 «Pagina completata»). **⚠ APOSTROPHE: all it JS values (KIND_IT + 27 strings + inline) 100% apostrophe-free** (KIND arts «una ghianda»/«un bottone» no elision; «Togline» clitic; «un {numeral}» no apostrophe; «è»/«più»/«à» = accents). SEO+prose JSON → U+2019 FINE. **⭐ GRADE = `GRADE_OVERRIDE it:'1'` classe PRIMA (pedagogue DECISIVE).** Base K; siblings de/fr/pt=1, es=PK. contare + SCRIVERE/produrre il numerale convenzionale 0–20 (incluso lo zero) = obiettivo classe-prima «Numeri» «leggere e SCRIVERE i numeri naturali» — l'infanzia registra con «simboli personali», NON il numerale (same boundary as digby #64); range-to-20 + teen (14, il passaggio alla decina) + zero-scritto exceed infanzia. Added to the GRADE_OVERRIDE row `{ de: '1', fr: '1', pt: '1' }` → `{ …, it: '1' }`; es='K' nativo diverges. **⭐ STRAND = «Numeri» — AUTO, NO it override (strand-names.ts C&C→it «Numeri»; NO rivets STRAND_OVERRIDE row exists anywhere — the "numeral built from objects" is fantasy decoration, NOT «Spazio e figure»).** `strand-names.ts` UNTOUCHED. **⭐ TERMINOLOGY (pedagogue+linguist):** Rivet «il procione» · forge «la fucina»/«Forgia!» (⚠ NOT «fucinare») · treasure «il tesoro»/«i tesori» · numeral **«il numero»** (⚠ pedagogue: NEVER «cifra» for K-1) · album «il tuo album» · pouch «il sacchetto». **2 terminology reconciliations (pedagogue-decisive, native-cultural):** pack **«la bustina»** (⚠ the Panini collector-card pack — thematically perfect with the album/carte da collezione; over the linguist's «pacchetto»; fem → «bustina vuota»/«riempi la bustina»/«farla combaciare»; applied to strings 4/8/12 + prose about[1]) + ticket **«il cartellino»** (⚠ a number-tag, semantically precise for "the ticket says 7"; over «biglietto»; strings 9/25). **Copywriter fix:** about[0] typo «il numeral»→«il numero». **REGISTER:** gender-neutral (never «Bravo/Brava»; «Sì!»/«Perfetto!»). SEO+prose JSON → U+2019 FINE. **⭐ GRADE = `GRADE_OVERRIDE it:'1'` classe PRIMA (pedagogue DECISIVE on a GENUINE FORK).** Base K; siblings de/pt=1, fr=GS, es=PK. The vocab (sopra/sotto/accanto/tra) IS infanzia-canonical, BUT this is **object-relative-to-object** placement (rispetto ad un ALTRO veicolo) on an ABSTRACT levelled diagram — NOT the embodied concrete infanzia «metti la palla sotto il tavolo» — mapping to the classe-prima «Spazio e figure» obiettivo «Comunicare la posizione di oggetti… rispetto ad altre persone o oggetti, usando termini adeguati» + the level-abstraction + «tra»/reverse. Skill = **leggere e regolare l'orologio analogico** (ore/mezze/quarti). 1.MD.B.3 machine anchor. **⚠ HEAVY TIME surface — each locale has its OWN clock-reading idiom function (`wordForDE` "Viertel nach/halb" · `wordForFR` "et quart/et demie" · `wordForES` "y cuarto/y media" · `wordForPT` "e quinze/e meia"). Rendered **DIGITAL «1:30»** (BOTH experts decisive; `wordForIT` guards `if (h === 1) return h + ':' + (m<10?'0':'') + m;` FIRST): «una e mezza» is grammatically WRONG (the hour «una» is obligatorily articled — «è l'una», never «è una»), so a digital fallback (one card of 14, analog clock beside) is the clean honest choice — the #62 clock-elapsed precedent. ⚠ **APOSTROPHE: all it JS values 100% apostrophe-free** — NO «l'una» (1:30→digital); NO «l'orologio»/«dell'» (aria «il quadrante»); ⚠ **setPrompt COLON dodge «Metti le lancette: {time}.»** (avoids «su le tre»/«sulle» preposition-article clash — {time} carries its own article «le tre»). - **#74 tempo-day-plan `time-prepositions.l-1-1-i` — «La giornata di Tempo» — 55th it, 1st it ELA/GRAMMAR — LOCAL, commit `e8d1af53`, NO deploy.** Pure activity-layer fan-out over the (already-generalized) time-preposition engine (0-touched `time-preposition-core.js` [FORMS=before/during/after, grade] + `tempo-day-plan-activity.js`, EN + de/fr/es/pt live). The turtle **Tempo** has a day plan; the child completes an everyday sentence by tapping ONE of 3 fixed temporal-preposition chips (before/during/after → prima/durante/dopo). 8 rounds prima×3/durante×3/dopo×2. Skill = **usare le preposizioni di tempo prima/durante/dopo**. L.1.1.i machine anchor. **⭐ ENGINE ARCHITECTURE (confirmed by reading core + siblings): a locale = (1) a `FORMS_xx` array of the 3 chip labels + (2) `roundsL10n.<loc>` in the MANIFEST whose per-round `correct` byte-matches a FORMS string; `_loadActivity` auto-selects `row.params.roundsL10n[LANG]` (falls back to `.rounds`); `tdpGrade`/`setupTask` dispatch on `LANG`.** Siblings `FORMS_DE=[vor/während/nach]` · `FR=[avant/pendant/après]` · `ES=[antes de/durante/después de]` · `PT=[antes da/durante a/depois da]`. **⭐⭐ THE ITALIAN CONTRACTION TRAP + pt-precedent solution:** «prima» obligatorily needs di+article-contraction («prima **della** corsa»=prima+di+la) while «durante»/«dopo» take the plain article — a FIXED chip set can't carry a per-round-varying article. As pt: **bake the article INTO the chips + keep every after-noun FEMININE + CONSONANT-INITIAL, written WITHOUT its article** → **FORMS_IT = ['prima della', 'durante la', 'dopo la']** + after-nouns corsa/recita/mensa/gita/canzone/festa/cena/passeggiata (all f, cons-initial). ⚠ **consonant-initial is LOAD-BEARING** — a vowel-initial feminine noun forces «la»→«l'» / «della»→«dell'» (apostrophe, banned in JS). Wrong chips still render parseably («durante la corsa» = grammatical, wrong-meaning — GOOD for ELA); the FULL-form chips force a MEANING choice (a bare "prima" against «___ la corsa» would be ungrammatical AND leak the article via «la» — pedagogue-validated as load-bearing). **⚠ APOSTROPHE: all it JS values 100% apostrophe-free** — FORMS + after-nouns clean by the feminine-cons-initial rule; the 7 strings/aria reworded 2 elisions (hintPick avoided «all'inizio»→«prima…alla fine»; hintWrong «all'ordine»→«quando succede»); «Sì»/«È» = accents not apostrophes. SEO+prose JSON → U+2019 FINE. **⭐ 8 rounds — linguist + pedagogue's 2 VERB FIXES (pedagogue-decisive):** r1 «Ci stiriamo»→**«Ci scaldiamo»** (*stirarsi*=iron oneself; *scaldiamo*=warm up, idiomatic pre-run); r2 «Stiamo zitti»→**«Stiamo in silenzio»** (*zitti* shush-edge vs the no-shame tone); r7 uses «cena» (f) not «pranzo» (m) to satisfy the feminine constraint for "before a meal". Rounds: scaldiamo|corsa|prima della · in silenzio|recita|durante la · riposiamo|mensa|dopo la · zaino|gita|prima della · mani|canzone|durante la · a-posto|festa|dopo la · laviamo-mani|cena|prima della · cantiamo|passeggiata|durante la. **⭐ GRADE = classe PRIMA (NO override — pedagogue DECISIVE + harmonizes all siblings).** Base "1"; page.tsx has ZERO tempo rows. Indicazioni: bare **USE** of a temporal connective to complete a sentence = classe prima (prima/dopo from scuola dell'infanzia; durante a small extension); the activity does NOT ask the child to metalinguistically *classify* prima/durante/dopo as preposizioni (that labelling = seconda/terza). So prima is harmonized AND independently correct → NO GRADE_OVERRIDE row. **⭐⭐ STRAND = «Riflessione linguistica» (NO override — pedagogue DECISIVE).** Manifest strand = `Language`; `strand-names.ts` ALREADY maps `'Language'.it = 'Riflessione linguistica'` (Indicazioni "Elementi di grammatica esplicita e riflessione sugli usi della lingua"). Connettivi/relazioni temporali = GRAMMAR → «Riflessione linguistica», NOT «Lessico» (Acquisizione ed espansione del lessico = content-word vocabulary — routing here would be a category error). `strand-names.ts` UNTOUCHED; NO page.tsx STRAND row. (First it ELA activity — a plain non-math strand; NO anti-calco override, unlike the M&D «Relazioni, dati e previsioni» cases #62/#65/#73.) **6 activity.js edits:** FORMS_IT (after FORMS_PT) + tdpGrade it arm + setupTask it choice-swap + speak() speechSynthesis `it → 'it-IT'` (LCSAudio `lang:LANG` → it auto) + turtle aria «Tempo la tartaruga» + 7 strings it. **7 strings:** title «La giornata di Tempo» · instruction «Tocca prima, durante o dopo per completare la frase.» · prompt «Tocca la parola del tempo giusta.» · tempoIntro «Alcune cose vengono prima, altre durante e altre dopo!» · hintPick «Succede prima, nel mezzo o alla fine?» · hintWrong «Pensa a quando succede: prima, durante o dopo?» · win «Sì! È il momento giusto. 🐢». L.2.4.a machine anchor. **⭐ ENGINE (like #75, DERIVED-ish): a round = `{id,band,word,sentence,choices:[{text,kind}]}`, kind ∈ correct (fits sentence) / no-context (another REAL meaning — the ambiguity trap) / off (unrelated wrong meaning); `grade = kind==='correct'`.** A locale = just `roundsL10n.<loc>` (de already made `_loadActivity` read `roundsL10n[LANG]` — UNTOUCHED) + 7 strings + fawn aria + speaker-button aria. - **🚫 #87 `compound-meaning.predict.l-2-4-d` = DEFERRED as DESIGN-NOT-FAN (operator-ratified 2026-08-26 via AskUserQuestion; matches es/fr/pt).** The compound-words engine (fishbowl = a bowl FOR fish) needs picturable N-N compounds where the whole is "a kind of the head". Italian — like es/fr/pt — has almost none of that shape; its productive compounds are **verb+noun** («portacenere», «cavatappi», «spazzaneve»), which aren't "a kind of" the noun and break the engine's head/supply/weld/predict cogs (`compound-meaning-core.js:139 predictShowsBothPartImages` requires 2 illustrable nouns). A faithful it build needs a NEW `compound-vn-core.js` engine (the es future-commission note), NOT a 0-core-line fan. **es/fr/pt ALL deferred it** (manifest has NO es/fr/pt roundsL10n). **DO NOT build as a fan; NO commit.**
